+ " (touch already lifted)"

+ "# Hit-Test Map Comparison"
+ "## Samples by difference"
+ "%#010llx Not starting drag: the mouse up was delivered before tracking began."
+ "%@ from edge with window size %@ start location %@ translation %@"
+ "%s : service did not include valid BOOL parameter in message for %@: invocation"
+ "%x not showing deferred expanded interface menu"
+ "%{public}@ data source %{public}@ implements -%{public}@ but not -%{public}@. Adopt the modern method to enable gesture-based drag and drop."
+ "%{public}s Didn't find an item awaiting action to perform"
+ "%{public}s Dismissing menu due to %s"
+ "%{public}s Drag/gesture event, cancel touch tracking moved out of menu, current point %@, touch-began %@, expandedMenuRect = [%@ - %@]"
+ "%{public}s Drag/gesture event, cancel touch tracking moving away from menu, current point %@, touch-began %@, expandedMenuRect = [%@ - %@]"
+ "%{public}s Menubar mouse down: setting didOpenMenuOnPreviousMouseDown=%s when starting session"
+ "%{public}s Menubar mouse down: setting didOpenMenuOnPreviousMouseDown=NO workaround for missed mouse-up"
+ "%{public}s Menubar mouse down: setting didOpenMenuOnPreviousMouseDown=YES because an unopen menu was hit"
+ "%{public}s Menubar mouse up for session %p, rootImpl %p, event.window=%p, didOpenMenuOnPreviousMouseDown=%s, _trackedImpl=%p, trackingMode=%@"
+ "%{public}s Menubar mouse up: setting didOpenMenuOnPreviousMouseDown=NO"
+ "%{public}s Performing item action '%@'"
+ "%{public}s Recording item action '%@' to perform on dismissal"
+ "%{public}s _asyncClient set: %{public}s"
